* Manually calculates the position (top value) of the date range overlay, while optionally updating that
* position if the date range picker component is inside a scrollable element.

* why calculate this manually? Since the overlay's position is "abosolute", it will be positioned relative to the
* first ancestor element who's position is not static. The typical way to do this would be to set the date range picker input to
* position: relative, which will position the overlay correctly, but if the input is being used inside an element
* that could have a scrollbar (like a sidebar) it will not clear the scrollbar and will instead for a horizontal
* scrollbar inside of its parent element, and looks terrible. With this approach, as long as the overlay is positioned
* to a non-static parent that is, or is an ancestor of, the element with the scrollbar, the overlay will be displayed
* over that scrollbar. But we now have to calculate the top value ourselves, as it will try to render its absolute
* position to the non-static parent instead of the date range picker's input

const ID_BASE = 'common-sidebar-nav';
const makeId = (idPostfix: string, idprefix?: string) =
idPrefix ? ‘$(idPrefix)-${ID_BASE)${idPostfix)’ : ‘${ID_BASE)$idPostfix)';


* The SidebarNav component contains all the open/close and tablet/mobile functionality out of the box
* while also being agnostic to what is displayed inside. Simply pass your desired components in
* as react children

* @required This component assumes it is being used inside of a flex container! It would be possible to
* override the css so that it does not use flex, however this is untested

* overriding the CommmonuI Theme Media Queries
* - If the need arises, you can override the media queries determing mobile/tablet mode through the
* WindowContext object. If no context is specified, mobile/tablet mode is determined by the media
* queries in the default commonui theme.

* custom styling
* Custom styling can be achieved by styling the specific classes inside of the component with css.
* NOTE: You can prefix these ids with the SidebarNav prop "idPrefix" in the case of multiple side bars
* on one page.
* - common-sidebar-nav - the root, and element that contains the nav + side bar open/close toggle
* - common-sidebar-nav-wrapper - The nav element containing the toggle header and/or content
* - common-sidebar-nav-toggle-header - the bar containing the title, toggle, and "x" button
* - common-sidebar-nav-toggle-open - The toggle bar that opens/toggles the nav bar
* - common-sidebar-nav-toggle-closed - the "x" button that closes the nav bar
* - common-sidebar-nav-content - the parent element of your content/the react children of the SidebarNav comp

typescript
export interface IsidebarNavprops

* If isstatic is false, the title to show next to the sidebar close toggle

title?: string:

* If sidebar is expandable, defines if sidebar should initially render as open or closed.
* Default is open (true)

initialExpandedstate?: boolean;

* Prefix added to each child components id for custom styling

* @example an idPrefix of "my-custom" yields "my-custom-common-side-nav-bar", etc.
* See SidebarNav docs for more information on styling with these ids

idprefix? : string:

* The width of the sidebar when not in tablet/mobile/small screen size mode

width?: string:

* The number of pixels to offset the height of the sidebar from the top of the view port.
* Can be used to line up the top of the sidebar with a horizontal nav bar running across the
* top of the page. If this value is not set or set to o, the sidebar will have a height of
* 1oovh, which if there is anything above it can extend the screen past the viewport and enable
* a scroll bar

* Additionaly in on tablet/mobile devices, this determines the gap between the top of viewport
* and the top of the sidebar header when expanded

* The css calculation looks like: calc(10ovh - $(offsetsidebarHeightPx)px)

offsetsidebarHeight?: string:

* If static is set, sidebar will not collapse or convert to a mobile/tablet bar.
* Defaults to false

isstatic? : boolean

* custom classes

className?: string

* Any content you need to display inside of the sidebar!

children: React.ReactNode

* Extra WAI-ARIA text for screen readers (optional).

arialabel?: string
.

const isComparisonRuleInput = (subcomponent: IChildRuleBuilderState): subcomponent is IComparisonRuleInputState =>
subcomponent.type === 'comparison';

const isRuleBuilderState = (subcomponent: IChildRuleBuilderState): subcomponent is IRuleBuilderState =>
subcomponent.type !== 'comparison';

export const getComparisonSubcomponents = (subcomponents: IChildRuleBuilderState[]) => {
return subcomponents.filter(isComparisonRuleInput);
};

export const getRuleBuilderState = (subcomponents: IChildRuleBuilderState[]) => {
return subcomponents.filter(isRuleBuilderState);
};

export const getSortedSubcomponents = (subcomponents: IChildRuleBuilderState[]) => {
const nonComparisonSubcomponents = subcomponents.filter((subcomponent) => subcomponent.type !== 'comparison');
const comparisonSubcomponents = getComparisonSubcomponents(subcomponents);
return [...comparisonSubcomponents, ...nonComparisonSubcomponents];
};

const getAdjacentSelectedSubcomponents = (subcomponents: IComparisonRuleInputState[]) => {
let tempGroup = [] as IComparisonRuleInputState[];
const adjacentSelectedRules = subcomponents.reduce((acc, curr, i) => {
if (!curr.checked) {
























const comparisonSubcomponents = getComparisonSubcomponents(subcomponents);
const adjacentSelectedSubcomponents = getAdjacentSelectedSubcomponents(comparisonSubcomponents);
return adjacentSelectedSubcomponents.some(
(subcomponentGroup) => subcomponentGroup[0]?.id?.toLowerCase() === id?.toLowerCase()
);
};

export const isPreGroupedSubcomponent = (subcomponents: IChildRuleBuilderState[], id?: string) => {
const comparisonSubcomponents = getComparisonSubcomponents(subcomponents);
const adjacentSelectedSubcomponents = getAdjacentSelectedSubcomponents(comparisonSubcomponents);
return adjacentSelectedSubcomponents.some((subcomponentGroup) =>
subcomponentGroup.map((subcomponent) => subcomponent.id).includes(id)
);
};

// splits out error key and pulls first numerical value which is always the top rule level index
export const getTopLevelRuleErrorState = (validationErrors: Record<string, string>, i: number) => {
return Object.keys(validationErrors)
.map((errorKey) => {
const splitKey = errorKey.split('-');
const parentRuleIndex = splitKey.find((element) => !Number.isNaN(+element));
return parentRuleIndex ? +parentRuleIndex : null;
})
.includes(i);

import { Alignment } from 'core';
import { IProductAccountFormSection } from 'types';

/** Form value can be a record of string:boolean if it's multi-select  */
/** I don't like using any, but in this case it's hard to avoid TS errors otherwise */
export type AccountFormValues = Record<string, any>;
export type AccountFormErrors = Record<string, boolean>;























* @file Local utils for SelectedProductAccountForm
*/
import { Dispatch, SetStateAction } from 'react';
import { IAOXOption, IAccountCard } from 'types';
import { AccountFormValues, AccountFormErrors } from './types';

export const sortOptions = (options: IAOXOption[]): IAOXOption[] =>
options.sort((a, b) => (a.order || 0) - (b.order || 0));

/**
* generateInitialAccountsFormData
* @summary
* Create initial data structure to track form values when there are multiple account cards, each with a form.
* Result is a map of { [account ID] : { [field ID] : [field value] } }
* @param cards array of @type {IAccountCard[]}
* @returns
*/
export const generateInitialAccountsFormData = (cards: IAccountCard[]): Record<string, Record<string, any>> => {
return cards.reduce(
(output, cardData) => ({
...output,
[cardData.accountId]: {},
}),
{}
























onChangeAccountsFormValues: (accountId: string, newFormValues: AccountFormValues) => void;
onChangeAccountsFormErrors: (accountId: string, newFormErrors: AccountFormErrors) => void;
onRemoveAccountsFormErrors: (accountIdArray: string[]) => void;
onRemoveAccountsFormValues: (accountIdArray: string[]) => void;
onRemoveAccountsFormData: (accountIdArray: string[]) => void;
} => {
const onChangeAccountsFormValues = (accountId: string, newFormValues: AccountFormValues): void => {
setAccountsFormValues((currentValue: Record<string, AccountFormValues>) => ({
...currentValue,
[accountId]: newFormValues,
}));
};
const onChangeAccountsFormErrors = (accountId: string, newFormErrors: AccountFormErrors): void => {
setAccountsFormErrors((currentValue: Record<string, AccountFormErrors>) => ({
...currentValue,
[accountId]: newFormErrors,
}));
};
const onRemoveAccountsFormErrors = (accountIdArray: string[]): void => {
setAccountsFormErrors((currentValue: Record<string, AccountFormErrors>) => {
const newValue = { ...currentValue };
accountIdArray.forEach((accountId: string) => {
delete newValue[accountId];
});

complexityRules?: ConsumerMgmtPasswordComplexityResponse
): PasswordValidations => {
const usernameSplitLeft = username.split('@')[0] ?? '';
const usernameSplitRight = username.split('@')[1]?.split('.')[0] ?? '';
const {
minLength = 8,
minLowerCase = 1,
minUpperCase = 1,
minNumber = 1,
minSymbol = 1,
excludeUsername = true,
excludeFirstName = true,
excludeLastName = true,
} = complexityRules ?? {};
return {
[PasswordRules.MIN_LENGTH]: {
message: nonHookTypedTranslation('SIGN_IN_WIZARD__PASSWORD_MIN_LENGTH', {
num: minLength,
}),
isValid: password.length >= minLength,
},
[PasswordRules.MIN_NUMBERS]: {
message: nonHookTypedTranslation('SIGN_IN_WIZARD__PASSWORD_MIN_NUMBERS', {
num: minNumber,
























message: nonHookTypedTranslation('SIGN_IN_WIZARD__PASSWORD_NO_USERNAME'),
isValid:
!excludeUsername || username === ''
? true
: !username.match(EMAIL_REGEX) ||
(!password.toLocaleLowerCase().includes(username.toLocaleLowerCase()) &&
!password.toLocaleLowerCase().includes(usernameSplitLeft.toLocaleLowerCase()) &&
!password.toLocaleLowerCase().includes(usernameSplitRight.toLocaleLowerCase())),
},
[PasswordRules.NO_FIRSTNAME]: {
message: nonHookTypedTranslation('SIGN_IN_WIZARD__PASSWORD_NO_FIRSTNAME'),
isValid:
!excludeFirstName || firstname === ''
? true
: !password.toLocaleLowerCase().includes(firstname.toLocaleLowerCase()),
},
[PasswordRules.NO_LASTNAME]: {
message: nonHookTypedTranslation('SIGN_IN_WIZARD__PASSWORD_NO_LASTNAME'),
isValid:
!excludeLastName || lastname === ''
? true
: !!lastname && !password.toLocaleLowerCase().includes(lastname.toLocaleLowerCase()),
},
};
























login: recoveryLogin = '',
firstName: recoveryFirstName = '',
lastName: recoveryLastName = '',
} = recoveryValidationCredentials || {};

  let passwordValidations: PasswordValidations = getPasswordValidations(
formData[SignInWizardForm.CREATE_USER][WizardFieldCode.PASSWORD],
formData[SignInWizardForm.CREATE_USER][WizardFieldCode.USERNAME],
formData[SignInWizardForm.CREATE_USER][WizardFieldCode.FIRSTNAME],
formData[SignInWizardForm.CREATE_USER][WizardFieldCode.LASTNAME],
complexityRules
);
switch (formContext) {
case SignInWizardForm.SIGN_IN:
newErrors[formContext] = clearFormErrors(formContext, newErrors);
if (!formData[formContext][WizardFieldCode.USERNAME].trim()) {
newErrors[formContext] = {
...newErrors[formContext],
[WizardFieldCode.USERNAME]: nonHookTypedTranslation('SIGN_IN_WIZARD__CANNOT_BE_BLANK'),
};
}
if (!formData[formContext][WizardFieldCode.PASSWORD]) {
newErrors[formContext] = {
...newErrors[formContext],
























[WizardFieldCode.USERNAME]: nonHookTypedTranslation('SIGN_IN_WIZARD__CANNOT_BE_BLANK'),
};
} else if (!formData[formContext][WizardFieldCode.USERNAME].match(EMAIL_REGEX)) {
newErrors[formContext] = {
...newErrors[formContext],
[WizardFieldCode.USERNAME]: nonHookTypedTranslation('SIGN_IN_WIZARD__EMAIL_MUST_BE_VALID'),
};
}

      if (!formData[formContext][WizardFieldCode.PASSWORD]) {
newErrors[formContext] = {
...newErrors[formContext],
[WizardFieldCode.PASSWORD]: nonHookTypedTranslation('SIGN_IN_WIZARD__CANNOT_BE_BLANK'),
};
} else if (Object.keys(passwordValidations).find((v) => !passwordValidations[v].isValid)) {
newErrors[formContext] = {
...newErrors[formContext],
[WizardFieldCode.PASSWORD]: nonHookTypedTranslation('SIGN_IN_WIZARD__PASSWORD_REQUIREMENTS'),
};
}

      if (
!Object.values(newErrors[formContext]).some((error) => !!error) &&
formData[formContext][WizardFieldCode.PASSWORD] !== formData[formContext][WizardFieldCode.PASSWORD_CONFIRM]
























};
}

      if (phoneNumber && phoneNumber.replace(/[^\d]/g, '').length !== 10) {
newErrors[formContext] = {
...newErrors[formContext],
[WizardFieldCode.PHONE_NUMBER]: nonHookTypedTranslation('SIGN_IN_WIZARD__INVALID_PHONE_NUMBER_LENGTH'),
};
}

      break;
}
case SignInWizardForm.RESET_PASSWORD:
newErrors[formContext] = clearFormErrors(formContext, newErrors);
if (!formData[formContext][WizardFieldCode.USERNAME]) {
newErrors[formContext] = {
...newErrors[formContext],
[WizardFieldCode.USERNAME]: nonHookTypedTranslation('SIGN_IN_WIZARD__CANNOT_BE_BLANK'),
};
}

      break;
case SignInWizardForm.CREATE_PASSWORD:
passwordValidations = getPasswordValidations(
























...newErrors[formContext],
[WizardFieldCode.PASSWORD_CONFIRM]: nonHookTypedTranslation('SIGN_IN_WIZARD__PASSWORDS_DONT_MATCH'),
};
}
break;
default:
break;
}
return { errors: newErrors, passwordValidations };
};

* Helper function to generate data array for SimpleAccordionGroup
* @param dataObject object that maps each accordion step (string) to content for that accordion
* @param {string[]} orderedSteps array of ordered accordion step strings
* @param {string} activeStep the active step of the accordion group
* @returns
*/
export const useGenerateAccordionGroupData = (
dataObject: Record<string, BaseAccordionGroupDataItem>,
orderedSteps: Array<string | IStepData>,
activeStep?: string
): Array<ISimpleAccordionDataItem> => {
const { t } = useTranslation();
const getStringStep = (input: string | IStepData): string => (typeof input === 'string' ? input : input.step);
const activeStepIndex = orderedSteps.findIndex((step) => getStringStep(step) === activeStep) || 0;
/** Created array of SimpleAccordionGroup data items */
const data = orderedSteps.map((step: string | IStepData, stepIndex: number) => {
let accordionStage = AccordionStage.UNACTIVE;
if (stepIndex === activeStepIndex) {
accordionStage = AccordionStage.ACTIVE;
} else if (stepIndex < activeStepIndex) {
accordionStage = AccordionStage.COMPLETED;
}
const stringStep = getStringStep(step);
const { accordionContent, ...contentRest } = dataObject[stringStep];

/* eslint no-param-reassign:0 no-restricted-properties:0 */
import { IRGBColor } from '.';

const convertHexToRgb = (hex: string): IRGBColor => {
const shorthandRegex = /^#?([a-f\d])([a-f\d])([a-f\d])$/i;
hex = hex.replace(shorthandRegex, (m, r, g, b) => {
return r + r + g + g + b + b;
});
























// read the colors and transform them into rgb format
const color1rgb = convertHexToRgb(foreGroundColor);
const color2rgb = convertHexToRgb(backgroundColor);
// calculate the relative luminance
// just checking for the r,g,b values as truthy makes 0 falsy. We want zeros.
if (
color1rgb.r !== undefined &&
color1rgb.g !== undefined &&
color1rgb.b !== undefined &&
color2rgb.r !== undefined &&
color2rgb.g !== undefined &&
color2rgb.b !== undefined
) {
const color1luminance = luminance(color1rgb.r, color1rgb.g, color1rgb.b);
const color2luminance = luminance(color2rgb.r, color2rgb.g, color2rgb.b);

    // calculate the color contrast ratio
const ratio =
color1luminance > color2luminance
? (color2luminance + 0.05) / (color1luminance + 0.05)
: (color1luminance + 0.05) / (color2luminance + 0.05);
return ratio;
}
return 0;

refreshToken: oktaCache.refreshToken,
codeVerifier: oktaCache.codeVerifier,
})};max-age=30;domain=cmgdlx.com;path=/;`;

.filter((cookie) => cookie.includes('oktaSession'))[0]
?.split('=')[1];

    if (oktaCookie && oktaCookie.length > 0) {
const { refreshToken, codeVerifier } = JSON.parse(oktaCookie);

if (refreshToken && codeVerifier) {
const { payload } = await API.refresh({ tenantId, refreshToken, codeVerifier });
LSService.setOktaCache(payload);
}
} catch (err) {

isUserAuthenticated,
routes,
};

</AuthenticationContext.Provider>
);
};

import { Base64 } from 'js-base64';
import { cloneDeep } from 'lodash';

/** Encodes primitives and object primitive values as Base64.
*
* This file is a mirror of EncodeValuesOnlyToBase64 with minor changes to style. For reference:
* https://dev.azure.com/cunamutual/H2IC/_git/CommonTS?path=/src/common/utils/EncodeValuesOnlyToBase64.ts
*
* Outputted format:  "base64,c3RyaW5nLHZhbHVlMQ=="
* The encoded part of the value is made up of th the primitive type of the value and the va    eaning "base64,Ym9vbGVhbixmYWxzZQ==" = "base64,<olean,false>"
* Example:
* This object:
* {
*    value1: "value1",
*    value2: false,
*    value3: 3,
*    value4: ["1", true, 3, { four: 4 }],
*    value5: {
*      one: "1",
*      two: true,
*      three: 3,
























if (valueToEncode === undefined || valueToEncode === null) return valueToEncode;

  let encodedValue = `${typeof valueToEncode},${valueToEncode.toString()}`;
encodedValue = `base64,${Base64.encode(encodedValue)}`;
return encodedValue;
}

/**
* This utility will encode the values of an object along with it's type to ensure that the value can be decoded back as the correct type
* @param objectToEncode - This is an unknown object.  The code doesn't care, because it will loop through the properties to figure out what to do.
* @returns a newly Base64 encoded object where the values of all of the properties have been converted to strings in the form of "Base64,<type,value>"
* @example
* {
*    value1: "value1",
*    value2: false,
*    value3: 3,
*    value4: ["1", true, 3, { four: 4 }]
}
* Encodes to:
* {
*    value1: "base64,c3RyaW5nLHZhbHVlMQ==",
*    value2: "base64,Ym9vbGVhbixmYWxzZQ==",
*    value3: "base64,bnVtYmVyLDM=",
*    value4: ["base64,c3RyaW5nLDE=", "base64,Ym9vbGVhbix0cnVl", "base64,bnVtYmVyLDM=", {four: "base64,bnVtYmVyLDQ="}]
























  return encodedObject;
}

/**
* This utility will encode the values of an object along with it's type to ensure that the value can be decoded back as the correct type
* @param arrayToEncode - This is an an array of unknown.  The code doesn't care about the types, because it will loop through the properties to figure out what to do.
* @returns a newly Base64 encoded array where the individual values, primitives or complex objects, have had the primitive values converted to strings in the form of "Base64,<type,value>"
* @example
*
* @param arrayToEncode
* @returns
*/
export function encodeArray(arrayToEncode: any[]): any[] {
const encodedArray = cloneDeep(arrayToEncode);
for (let i = 0; i < encodedArray.length; i++) {
if (typeof encodedArray[i] === 'object') {
encodedArray[i] = encodeObject(encodedArray[i]);
} else {
encodedArray[i] = encodeValue(encodedArray[i]);
}
}

























* Deserializes `body` and then encodes it's values as base64 strings before
* reserializing them. Useful in openapi-generator middlewares.
*
* @export
* @param {string} body
* @return {*}  {string}
*/
export function encodeStringBody(body: string): string {
const parsed = JSON.parse(body);
const encoded = encodeObject(parsed);
return JSON.stringify(encoded, null, '');
}

import { DateTime } from 'luxon';

export default (dateToFormat: string | undefined): string =>
dateToFormat

month: 'long',
year: 'numeric',
day: 'numeric',
})
: '';

/**
*
* @param myDate date string you want to compare to now()
* @param showRelative optional defaults to false - determines whether the function returns time from now or just a datetime
* Returns formatted datetime or 'time ago' strings
*/
export const formatDatetime = (
myDate: string,
showRelative: boolean = false,
separateDayTime: boolean = false,
separateDayShort: boolean = false
) => {
























if (datetime > now.minus({ weeks: 1 }) && datetime < now.plus({ weeks: 1 })) {
return `${datetime.toFormat('MMM d')} at ${datetime.toFormat('h:mm a')}`;
}
return `${datetime.toFormat('MMM d, yyyy')} at ${datetime.toFormat('h:mm a')}`;
}

  if (separateDayShort) {
const formatted = separateDayTime ? '' : datetime.toFormat('MM/dd/yyyy [at] h:mm a');
return formatted.replace('AM', 'a.m.').replace('PM', 'p.m.');
}

  if (datetime.hasSame(now, 'day')) return `Today, ${datetime.toFormat('h:mm a')}`;
if (datetime.hasSame(now.plus({ days: 1 }), 'day')) return `Tomorrow, ${datetime.toFormat('h:mm a')}`;
if (datetime.hasSame(now.minus({ days: 1 }), 'day')) return `Yesterday, ${datetime.toFormat('h:mm a')}`;
if (datetime > now.minus({ weeks: 1 }) && datetime < now.plus({ weeks: 1 })) {
return `${datetime.toFormat('MMM d')}, ${datetime.toFormat('h:mm a')}`;
}
return `${datetime.toFormat('MMM d, yyyy')}, ${datetime.toFormat('h:mm a')}`;
};

return useCallback(async () => {
try {
if (isOauth2) {
await userLogout({ token: token as string });
authDispatch?.(authActions.clearUserToken());
LSService.removeItem(LSService.CONSTANTS.OAUTH2_TOKEN_STORAGE);
push(routes.loginRedirect);

/* eslint-disable no-param-reassign */
import { IConditionProperties, IFact } from 'core/entities/decisioning';
import * as Yup from 'yup';

export const ConditionErrors = {
number: 'must be a number',
percentage: 'must be a two decimal percentage',
string: 'must be a non empty string',
select: 'must select a valid value',
range: (start: string, end: string) => `must be a range between ${start} and ${end}`,
};

const handleValidation = (
condition: IConditionProperties,
errorText: string,
validation: { isValidSync: (value: any) => boolean } | boolean
) => {
const result = typeof validation === 'boolean' ? validation : validation.isValidSync(condition.value);
if (result || !condition.isActive) {
delete condition['errorText'];
} else {
condition.errorText = errorText;
}
























* @param facts list of facts that are apart of the rule the condition is in, used to get the fact type
* @returns boolean representing if the condition was validated or not.
*
* Note this function assumes we are using the immer library, so the condtion object is itself being modified and
* immer will handle the state management
*/
export const defaultConditionValidator = (
condition: IConditionProperties,
previousCondition: IConditionProperties,
facts: IFact[]
): boolean => {
// Clear error if no fact was set
if (condition.fact === '') {
handleValidation(condition, '', true);
return true;
}
if (condition.fact !== previousCondition.fact) {
handleValidation(condition, '', true);
return true;
}
const conditionFact = facts.find((f) => f.fact === condition.fact);
if (!conditionFact) {
throw new Error(`Trying to validate a fact that is not in the facts list: ${condition.fact}`);
}

























// The below functions are a way to allow us to replace/extend the validation from outside the CommonUI library.  We want
// some validation here, but the better place for all validation to live is probably AstroUI.
let customConditionValidator: typeof defaultConditionValidator | undefined;

export const setCustomConditionValidator = (validator: typeof defaultConditionValidator) => {
customConditionValidator = validator;
};

export const getConditionValidator = () => {
if (customConditionValidator) {
return customConditionValidator;
}
return defaultConditionValidator;
};

```

// Compound nodes with a single child node render as a single, so need to catch that and update the single condition node
// in its node list, rather than updating a single condition node directly
if (toUpdate.type === 'compound') {
if (toUpdate.nodes.length !== 1) {
throw new Error('Attempting to update a compound node that has more than one child');
}
conditionValidator(newCondition, toUpdate.nodes[0].condition, facts);
toUpdate.nodes[0].condition = newCondition;
return;
}
if (toUpdate.type !== 'single') {
throw new Error(`Depth map did not map to a valid leaf node: ${JSON.stringify({ draft, index, depthMap: m })}`);
}

import { LogicalGroupNode, ConditionNode } from 'core/entities/decisioning';
import { SetNodeCallback } from 'components/organisms/Rule';

export const setNodesRecursively = (node: ConditionNode | LogicalGroupNode, processNode: SetNodeCallback) => {
switch (node.type) {
case 'single':
processNode(node);
break;
case 'compound':
case 'logicGroup':
node.nodes.forEach((n) => setNodesRecursively(n, processNode));
break;
default:
throw new Error(`Invalid logic type: ${(node as ConditionNode).type}`);
}
};

```

type HookPayload = () => boolean;

export const useMounted = (): HookPayload => {
const mountedRef = useRef<boolean>(false);
const isMounted = useCallback(() => mountedRef.current, []);
useEffect(() => {
mountedRef.current = true;

    return (): void => {
mountedRef.current = false;
};
});

  return isMounted;
};

export const TestComponent = (): JSX.Element => {
const isMounted = useMounted();

  return <div>{isMounted() ? 'TRUE' : 'FALSE'}</div>;
};

import { useEffect } from 'react';

export const useRestoreScroll = (x = 0, y = 0): void => {
useEffect(() => {
window.scrollTo(x, y);
}, [x, y]);
};

* three part naming convention;
* 1) First part, the component name, in all caps snake case, where it is used ( example: BEFORE_UNLOAD_MODAL )

* 3) Third part is the description, also in all caps snake case.

import { SignInWizardSuccess } from 'components/organisms';
import { LocalStorageBackupValues } from 'commonui-shared';

interface IStorageService {
getItem: (name: string) => string | null;
set: (name: string, value: string) => void;
removeItem: (name: string) => void;
clear: () => void;
}

export const storagePrimary: IStorageService = {
getItem: (name: string): string | null => {
return window.localStorage.getItem(name);
},
set: (name: string, value: string): void => {
window.localStorage.setItem(name, value);
},
removeItem: (name: string): void => {
window.localStorage.removeItem(name);
},
























export const retryWithBackup = <T>(action: (storage: IStorageService) => T): T => {
try {
return action(storageRef);
} catch (e: any) {
if (e instanceof DOMException && storageRef === storagePrimary) {
// switch to backup storage and retry
storageRef = storageBackup;
return action(storageRef);
}
throw e;
}
};

export const set = (name: string, value: string): void => retryWithBackup(({ set }) => set(name, value));
export const getItem = (name: string): string | null => retryWithBackup(({ getItem }) => getItem(name));
export const removeItem = (name: string): void => retryWithBackup(({ removeItem }) => removeItem(name));
export const clearAll = (): void => retryWithBackup(({ clear }) => clear());

export const CONSTANTS = {
ALERT__SIM_SUPPRESS_ADJUSTMENT_GROWL: 'alert_sim_suppressAdjustmentGrowl',
MYLOAN_GET_ACCESS_MEMBERSHIP_GROWL: 'myloan-get-access-membership-growl',

refreshToken: input.refreshToken,

userEmail: input.userEmail,

* This should be placed at the top of the rendering tree
*
* Applies a display none to all links and buttons when printing
*

*
* @todo flexbox does not behave well on print and display modes should most likely be converted to block
*/

@media print {
background: transparent;
button,
a {
display: none;
outline: none !important;

import { palette } from './default';
import { IFontName } from './fontStyles';

export type Mode = 'DARK' | 'LIGHT';

export const modeStyles: Partial<Record<IFontName, Record<Mode, string>>> = {
title4: {
DARK: `color: ${palette.contrastWhite}`,
LIGHT: `color: ${palette.contrastBlack}`,
},
title3: {
DARK: `color: ${palette.contrastWhite}`,
LIGHT: `color: ${palette.contrastBlack}`,
},
body3: {
DARK: `color: ${palette.contrastGreyscale04}`,
LIGHT: `color: ${palette.contrastGreyscale08}`,
},
























}

export enum DisplayMode {
DARK = 'DARK',
LIGHT = 'LIGHT',
}

export enum Opacity {
'30%' = 0.3,
'40%' = 0.4,
'50%' = 0.5,
'60%' = 0.6,
'80%' = 0.8,
'85%' = 0.85,
}

export enum Shape {
Rounded = 'rounded',
Soft = 'soft',
}

export type IOpacity = keyof typeof Opacity;
export type IPropsHook = (props: Record<string, any>) => string;

























* Returns a color value from our theme definition
*/
static getColor(themeColorName: IThemeColors): IPropsHook {
return (props): string => props.theme && props.theme[themeColorName];
}

  static getModeColor({ dark, light }: { dark: IThemeColors; light: IThemeColors }): IPropsHook {
return (props): string => props.mode && props.theme && props.theme[props.mode === DisplayMode.DARK ? dark : light];
}

  /**
* Returns @media-query string
* e.g. '@media-query ((min-width: 0px) and (max-width: 379px))
* or ((min-width: 380px) and (max-width: 767px))'
*/
static getMediaQuery(...screenSizes: ScreenSize[]): IPropsHook {
return (props: Record<string, any>): string => {
const breakpoints = (props.theme && props.theme.breakpoints) || [1, 2, 3];
if (screenSizes.length === 0) throw new Error('wrong screen size');
if (screenSizes.length === 1) {
return `@media ${Theme.getMinMaxSize(screenSizes[0], breakpoints)}`;
}
const sizes = screenSizes.map((size) => Theme.getMinMaxSize(size, breakpoints));
return `@media ${sizes.join(',')}`;
























case ScreenSize.XL:
minWidth = breakpoints[2];
break;
default:
return 'wrong screen size';
}
mediaQuery = `(min-width: ${minWidth}px)`;
if (maxWidth > 0) {
mediaQuery += ` and (max-width: ${maxWidth}px)`;
}
return mediaQuery;
}

  /**
* Returns number from the breakpoint array
* e.g. 1024
*/
static getBreakpoint(screenSize: ScreenSize): number {
let newBreakpoint;
switch (screenSize) {
case ScreenSize.SM:
newBreakpoint = defaultTheme.breakpoints[0];
break;
case ScreenSize.MD:
























* @param mode DARK | LIGHT
*/
static getModeStyles(fontName: IFontName, mode: Mode): FlattenSimpleInterpolation {
const styles = modeStyles[fontName]?.[mode];
return css`
${styles || ''}
`;
}

* dropdown bar.
* e.g. ${getGlobalHeaderHeight('globalNavbar')}
* @param heightName
*/
static getGlobalHeaderHeight(heightName: IThemeColors): IPropsHook {
return (props): string => props.theme && props.theme[heightName];
}
